# A1 Vocabulary List (Wortschatz)

A curated collection of authentic, level-appropriate German vocabulary for the **A1 (Beginner / Anfänger)** level according to the CEFR standards. Each entry includes the word with grammatical markers, English definition, topic category, a contextual example sentence with English translation, and direct reference links.

[A1 Grammatik](./Grammatik.md) | [Verbs](./Verbs.md) | [Nouns](./Nouns.md) | [Adjectives](./Adjectives.md) | [Cases](./Cases.md) | [Prepositions](./Prepositions.md) | [Pronouns](./Pronouns.md)
